- **thread-to-pdf**: Render an email thread to a single paginated PDF with correct bidirectional text — Hebrew and Arabic messages come out right-to-left, English ones left-to-right, in the same document. Use for archiving correspondence, sending a chain to a lawyer or regulator, or producing a readable record from loose .eml files. Triggers on "PDF of this email chain", "print this thread", "make a PDF of these emails", "RTL email PDF", "Hebrew email to PDF". - **tic-lookup**: Transparency in Coverage (TiC) price lookup tool for querying CMS machine-readable in-network rate files. Use this skill whenever the user asks about insurance reimbursement rates, negotiated rates, payer pricing, price transparency data, facility rate comparisons, or wants to look up what insurance plans pay for specific CPT/HCPCS codes. Also trigger when the user mentions TiC files, in-network rates, price transparency, payer/plan rate lookups, or wants to compare reimbursement across hospitals, ASCs, or providers. Even if the user just asks "what does Aetna pay for a knee replacement" or "… ([recursor/INOV8Skills](https://github.com/recursor/INOV8Skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/transparency-in-coverage-skills/skills/tic-lookup) · ★1)
- **tic-peak-boundary-estimation**: Use when after sliding-window analysis has identified candidate TIC peaks but before parameter extraction from Extracted Ion Chromatograms (EICs). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/tic-peak-boundary-estimation) · ★15 · 2 copies)
- **tic-peak-identification-sliding-window**: Use when you have loaded raw mass spectrometry data (mzML, mzXML, or CDF format) into AutoTuner and need to identify peak regions in the TIC trace prior to extracted ion chromatogram (EIC) analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/tic-peak-identification-sliding-window) · ★15 · 2 copies)
- **tic-peak-isolation-validation**: Use when you have raw mass spectrometry data (mzML, mzXML, or CDF format) from at least 3 samples and need to automatically identify candidate peak regions in the TIC chromatogram before extracting ion-level parameters for XCMS or MZmine2 processing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/tic-peak-isolation-validation) · ★15 · 2 copies)
- **tic-tac-toe-game**: Deterministic Tic-Tac-Toe game engine evaluating every AI move through explicit Tree-of-Thought branching with Win/Threat/Control scoring, delivering only two sections per turn with zero conversational filler. ([princemanjee/EngineeredPromptLibrary](https://github.com/princemanjee/EngineeredPromptLibrary/tree/HEAD/plugins/pl-roleplay-games/skills/tic-tac-toe-game) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **ticaret-sicili-ve-aleniyet**: Tescil, ilan ve sicil kaydinin ucuncu kisilere etkisi, gorunuse guven ve tescile davet/itiraz gerektiginde; sicildeki yanlis veya eksik kayitlarin sorumluluk dogurup dogurmadigini degerlendirmek icin kullanilir. ([aydincan/turk-hukuku-ve-claude](https://github.com/aydincan/turk-hukuku-ve-claude/tree/HEAD/ticari-isletme-hukuku/skills/ticaret-sicili-ve-aleniyet) · ★11)
- **ticaret-unvani-ve-isletme-adi**: Ticaret unvaninin olusturulmasi, zorunlu/secimlik ekler, unvanin korunmasi ve unvana tecavuz; benzer unvan veya isletme adi kullanimina karsi tespit-men-tazminat talepleri gerektiginde kullanilir. ([aydincan/turk-hukuku-ve-claude](https://github.com/aydincan/turk-hukuku-ve-claude/tree/HEAD/ticari-isletme-hukuku/skills/ticaret-unvani-ve-isletme-adi) · ★11)
- **ticari-dava-gorev-arabuluculuk**: Bir uyusmazligin ticari dava olup olmadigini, asliye ticaret mahkemesinin gorevli olup olmadigini, dava sarti arabuluculugun zorunlu olup olmadigini ve yetkili mahkemeyi belirlemek gerektiginde kullanilir. ([aydincan/turk-hukuku-ve-claude](https://github.com/aydincan/turk-hukuku-ve-claude/tree/HEAD/ticari-isletme-hukuku/skills/ticari-dava-gorev-arabuluculuk) · ★11)
- **ticari-elektronik-ileti-iys**: SMS, e-posta veya arama yoluyla gönderilen ticari iletilerde onay, ret hakkı ve İleti Yönetim Sistemi (İYS) yükümlülüklerinin denetlenmesi ya da bir yaptırım/şikâyet savunması hazırlanması gerektiğinde kullanılır. ([aydincan/turk-hukuku-ve-claude](https://github.com/aydincan/turk-hukuku-ve-claude/tree/HEAD/e-ticaret-hukuku/skills/ticari-elektronik-ileti-iys) · ★11)
- **ticari-islerde-faiz-ve-temerrut**: Bir ticari alacakta uygulanacak faiz turunu ve oranini (ticari temerrut faizi, avans faizi, kapital faizi), faiz baslangic tarihini ve bilesik faiz sinirlarini belirlemek gerektiginde kullanilir. ([aydincan/turk-hukuku-ve-claude](https://github.com/aydincan/turk-hukuku-ve-claude/tree/HEAD/ticari-isletme-hukuku/skills/ticari-islerde-faiz-ve-temerrut) · ★11)
- **ticari-sozlesme-ve-belge-taslagi**: Tacirler arasi cerceve/mal alim/hizmet sozlesmesi, cari hesap sozlesmesi, fatura itiraz veya temerrut ihtarnamesi ile ticaret sicili basvurusu gibi belgelerin TTK emredici hukumlerine uygun taslagini uretmek gerektiginde kullanilir.
